**Session summary (2026-11-02 — NCAAB moneyline model, LIVE: `ncaab_moneyline_own_spread`):**

mike: *"we need to build 1H and game lines ML and Spread no matter what"*, *"we do not need to wait 10 games"*, *"I want it all done and go live now"*, and the cap *"-200 max"*. Built from the round-4 moneyline research, on top of the spread model (not yet merged; this branch sits on its commit). At 15:00 UTC on game day each of the eight bettable books' full-game moneyline is priced off THAT book's own full-game spread at the same instant: the spread's prices power de-vigged to a cover chance, the final-margin distribution turned around to an expected margin, then P(win). Moneylines are left out when the implied sum is outside 1.00-1.15, when the book's favourite differs from every other book's (Pinnacle and Bovada counted), or when the book's own spread (1.5 or more) names the other favourite. Every side shorter than -200 is removed before the pick; the single largest edge of the game is bet when above 0.01. Decided once per game; a missed 15:00 is recorded as `missed` and fails the run.

**What changed.** New: `models/ncaab_moneyline_own_spread.py` (the read, flags, pricing, pick, decision; the research's power de-vig ported unchanged), `scripts/ncaab_moneyline_card.py` (its own card and job; ledger `ncaab_moneyline_decisions`; picks at the bet book's price, `scored_line` and `line_book` empty, DraftKings' clean moneyline as the reference), `scripts/ncaab_moneyline_build.py` (refits the margin tables with the spread build's own functions, refuses to write unless the 2027 table equals the spread artifact's, replays the card's path walk-forward), `models/saved/ncaab_moneyline_own_spread_2027.json` (5 KB: it names the spread artifact's table and pins its SHA-256 instead of copying 1.2 MB), `tests/test_ncaab_moneyline_own_spread.py`, `tests/test_ncaab_moneyline_card_pg.py`. Extended additively: `models/ncaab_book_quotes.py` (`books` and `need_line` arguments, an `h2h` market with no line). Registered: config (cut 0.01, own EV floor 0.015, price floor -200, own probability, rule), settlement `h2h`, `clv_game_market` names it (stamp bumped to `...-ncaab-moneyline-own-spread`), the Claude-mobile prompt, the line-movement check (comment), heartbeat, worker jobs, worker-only table, scheduler (hourly :05 UTC, its own job), the app (label "NCAAB Moneyline" / ML, market, page copy, posted-once note), the generated thresholds, docs.

**The id has 'spread' in it.** The app's `gameMarketForModel` and `marketClassForModel` and the Claude-mobile prompt map any id containing "spread" to the spreads market; each now names this id as a moneyline first, pinned by tests. The production view `v_model_full_record` (read by nothing in the repo) maps it to spreads; with no line, that view leaves these bets out rather than mis-grading them. Not changed.

**Judgement calls.** (1) Its own job and card, not the spread card's, so each model keeps its own kill switch, heartbeat and failure, and the spread code (another session's unmerged commit) is untouched; the card imports the spread card's slate. (2) The margin table is referenced, not copied. (3) A game tipping less than five minutes after 15:00 UTC is not priced, the research's rule. (4) DraftKings' price is the reference only when its 15:00 moneyline is unflagged. (5) The brief said the rule was chosen from 54 versions; `floor200_grid.csv` holds 162 (18 rule variants x 9 cuts), and the docs say 162.

**Verified.** The build on the research's copy of the Supabase reads: walk-forward tables equal the research's (largest difference 0.0, 2021-2026), and the 2027 table equals the spread artifact's (0.0). The replay of the card's own path matched all 914 research bets on game, side, price and book, edge difference 0.0, none missing, none added. 2021-2025 838 bets 511-327 +53.11 units +4.22% [-1.2, 9.3]; 2025-26 76 bets 45-31 +14.04 units +15.05% [-4.9, 32.3]; by season +5.53%, +4.90%, +5.42%, +2.71%, +1.98%, +15.05%. Smallest EV admitted +0.0156. Production reads (read-only): every NCAAB final in seasons 2025-2027 has `home_win` set and consistent with the score; the 2026-27 slate stores h2h from all eight books plus Pinnacle and Bovada. Watched the floor tests fail with the pre-pick floor removed.

**UX designer review.** First pass: ship with two copy fixes, both applied. The model's page body now says what it compares (each book's moneyline against the chance that same book's spread implies; the bet is at the book where the gap is largest), and the spread model's body is unchanged. The card's "record uses best price" and Pick Detail's "best price across all books" are false for this model on 269 of its 914 replayed bets (a book another bettable book beat on price at 15:00 UTC, measured on the research's candidate quotes), so `mobile/src/lib/myBooks.ts` scopes them: this model says "record kept at that price" / "the price the model found, not your book's", every other model keeps the existing wording. A probability note says where the chance comes from. Second pass: ship. The same best-price sentence in the Explainer and Settings screens is left for the owner to decide. `verify_ncaab_ui.ts`, `verify_pick_timing.ts`, `verify_my_books.ts` pass; `verify_preferred_book.ts` fails one check ("every ingested book is carried") on the base commit too. A type-check of the five changed app files gives the base commit's error set, none in the three library files.

**Not done.** No production write, no worker job queued, no Odds API call, not merged. It did not clear the research bar (interval below zero; close value about -0.2% / -1.4% against Pinnacle), and the docs say so.

**Session summary (2026-11-02 — NCAAB spread model, LIVE: `ncaab_spread_market`):**

mike: *"we need to build 1H and game lines ML and Spread no matter what"*, and *"we do not need to wait 10 games ... I want these models live now"*. Built from the round-4 research (verified line shopping at 15:00 UTC). At 15:00 UTC on game day each of the eight bettable books' full-game spread is read (newest pre-game row stamped in the hour before), pick'em sign errors are dropped, the consensus is the median line (the book priced included), the expected home margin is minus the consensus plus +0.2494 (November) / -0.0815 (December on), and every quote is priced with the empirical final-margin distribution. The best quote is bet when its edge (P(win, push left out) minus its own price's implied) is above 0.03 and 3+ other books quote the game. No games-played wait. Model update: `Updated-By: mike`.

**What changed.** New: `models/ncaab_margin_dist.py` (the distribution, a port of the research kernel), `models/ncaab_book_quotes.py` (the 15:00 UTC multi-book read: window, artefact flag, consensus, and the one SQL read), `models/ncaab_spread_market.py` (pricing, best quote, the decision), `scripts/ncaab_spread_card.py` (the card: ledger `ncaab_spread_decisions`, picks at the bet book's line and price, missed-check failure), `scripts/ncaab_spread_market_build.py` (fits and freezes `models/saved/ncaab_spread_market_2027.json`, replays the card's path walk-forward), `tests/test_ncaab_spread_market.py`, `tests/test_ncaab_spread_card_pg.py`. Registered beside `ncaab_total_blend` everywhere: config (cut 0.03, prob 0.0, EV floor 0.05, price floor -200, own probability, rule), settlement map (spreads), the closing-line market function (stamp bumped to `...-ncaab-spread-market`, because production still held `...-nba-first-half-total-luck` and mapped the id to h2h), the line-movement check, the heartbeat (150 min), the worker job set, worker-only tables, the scheduler job (hourly :05 UTC), the app label ("NCAAB Spreads") and its page and pick timing copy, the generated thresholds mirror, and the tests that list rule models.

**Judgement calls.** (1) The offset c is the research's constant from the 2012-2020 archive, frozen. The build brief described a refit on 2021..S-1; the research never refit it, and a refit (+0.31 / -0.02 on the 2021-2026 15:00 UTC consensus) is untested. (2) The quote window is 60 minutes, the research's rule, not the totals card's 90. (3) Hourly job rather than one 15:05 job, so a missed 15:00 is written as `missed` and fails the next run; nothing is ever bet on a later quote. The card's very first run does not fail for games whose 15:00 came before it existed. (4) A bet at another book's line leaves `dk_odds` empty and names the book in `line_book` (the scorer's off-book convention); DraftKings' price goes in `dk_*` only when DraftKings had the same line.

**Verified.** The build on the research's copy of the Supabase reads: the walk-forward distributions equal the research's (largest difference 0.0, 2021-2026); c reproduced (+0.2494 / -0.0815); the replay of the card's own path matched all 613 research bets on game, side, line and price, the same book on all 613. Live version (3+ other books): 2021-2025 409 bets, +44.60 units, +9.97% [0.8, 18.4]; 2025-26 133 bets, +30.34 units, +20.96%. Without the minimum: 478 / +57.23 / +10.97% and 135 / +32.34 / +22.01%. Production read (light, by game id): at 15:00 UTC on 2026-11-02 the median quote was 42 minutes old (the 9:17 ET refresh pass), 83-96% of book-and-game pairs fresh across the day's hours, Caesars missing 14:00-16:00 UTC. Tests: 37 unit + 3 real-Postgres; watched fail under ten mutations (window 90, no stamp bound, in-play rows, other markets, no artefact rule, no 3-book minimum, a tie to the away side, no missed failure, deciding after 15:00, DraftKings' price on another line). UX review (`frontend-ux-designer`): ship with fixes, all applied. The model joins `BET_BOOK_LINE_MODELS` (read as DraftKings' line, a point moved against the bet showed as a green price move; `tests/test_mobile_team_total.py` now covers it home and away, watched failing first), the page copy names "each book we price" and that the bet may not be at DraftKings, and the empty state says "by noon ET". The custom-model builder gains an NCAAB "Spread" row (any id with `_spread` lands there, as NCAAF's does); `verify_ncaab_ui.ts` now pins that row. All 75 app verify scripts: the same 53 failing check lines on the branch and on master, none new. Type check (tsc over the touched app files): the same 10 errors on the branch and master, all from type packages this machine lacks; none new. Full suite (`python -m pytest -q tests/`): branch 8 failed, 8,902 passed, 69 skipped; clean origin/master `efc830ca` 12 failed, 8,853 passed, 69 skipped. Every branch failure is in master's set (zero new); master's other four pass when run alone (they depend on test order). Production dry run, read-only, as of 15:05 UTC on 2026-11-02 (no row it read was stored after 15:05): 179 games due, 116 quoted, 9 bets, none refused by the floors (listed in `docs/sports/ncaab.md`).

**Not done.** No production write, no worker job queued, no Odds API call, not merged. The fill rate of a 15:00 off-market number is unmeasured; the ledger keeps every book's quote so it can be.

**Session summary (2026-11-03 — Teams board: every stat explained, NBA/NHL season fixed, NBA ratings on the worker, MLB main run line):**

Matt: "I don't really know how each is helpful for a user to use that data for betting ... explain each data field". Approved: casual-but-serious tone, rearrange the tabs, splits under 10 games show the record, fix the bugs found.

- **Copy.** Every stat in `mobile/src/lib/teamStatCatalog.ts` carries a one-line `hint` (always on screen) and an `explain` (what it is / what's good / how to use it / watch out for) behind "Details" (`InfoTooltip` gained headed `sections`). The Betting tab keeps an always-visible caveat line; the other tabs' intro opens each sheet. Before: 10 of 35 stats had a hint, none said how to use the number. No copy states an unmeasured number (wRC+ 100 is the definition; 52.4% at -110 is 110/210). Two unmeasured claims in the old hints were dropped ("the half of the staff the market prices least efficiently", "a documented, repeatable effect").
- **Tabs.** Scored/Allowed/Margin per game moved Efficiency → Record (unadjusted); NFL Pass/Rush Yds/G moved to Record (volume follows game script). MLB/NHL betting labels say Run Line / Puck Line (`bySport`, `spreadWord`).
- **Small samples.** `MIN_SPLIT_SAMPLE` 8 → 10; `sample` is summed columns (ATS: w+l+p, O/U: o+u+p, home/away: decided home/away games, rest: its count). A thin split shows its record ("3-2", "3o-2u") or its game count, never a percentage, untinted, ranked below every split with enough games. Board and team page apply the same rule. Fav/dog have no count column in `team_stats_board`, so they cannot apply it; their copy says so.
- **Bug: NBA/NHL showed last season.** The board asked for the calendar year (2026); NBA/NHL label by ending year, so on 2026-11-02 it showed the finished 2025-26 season labelled "2026". `teamBoardSeason` (ET clock) asks 2027 and the header reads "2026-27". `fetchTeamStats` now falls back one label for every sport.
- **Bug: no NBA 2026-27 efficiency.** `nba_team_stats` held no season 2027 row (last as_of 2026-08-30): its only writer is the stats.nba.com job on Matt's machine. New `data/ingestors/nba_team_stats_from_box.py` builds it on the worker from `nba_player_game_log` (ESPN) + `games` finals, step `nba-team-stats-box` in the 6am run before `refresh-team-board`. ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING, so a stats.nba.com row still wins its key. No live model reads the table (the NBA game models are retired).
- **Bug: MLB ATS graded at alternates.** `team_stats_board_compute` took DraftKings' newest pre-game `spreads` row whatever its number; 2 of 40 finished MLB games from 2026-09-15 were graded at ±2.5. `team_stats_board_main_run_line_2026_11_03.sql` patches the LIVE function text (marker `team_board_main_run_line`) to take ±1.5 only for MLB/NHL spreads.

**Verified.** Box scores: 184 of 184 NBA 2026-27 team-games sum to the final score. Method vs the source it replaces (season 2026, 30 teams, rebuilt from that season's player logs vs stored stats.nba.com): Net Rtg corr 0.9999; Off/Def Rtg MAE 0.79/0.80 with +0.79 bias (no team turnovers in player boxes, cancels in Net Rtg); pace 0.71; eFG% 0.0002. Read-only dry run of the new query for 2027 as of 2026-11-03: 30 teams, 4–7 games, mean Net Rtg −0.19. Run line: the 1.5-only probe found a 1.5 row for 40 of 40 sampled MLB games (no game loses its grade); NHL 60 of 60 already 1.5; the anchor appears exactly twice in the live definition. `tests/test_team_stats_explained.py` 8 passed, all 8 fail on origin/master; removing the thin-ranking line fails the ranking test. `verify_team_board.ts` 89 passed. tsc error set byte-identical to master (30; the stale `queries.ts` baseline entry is stale on master too). ux_scan 0 findings.

**UX review** (`frontend-ux-designer`): ship with fixes. Applied: intro row Betting-only and into the sheet body otherwise; no line cap on it; thin no-record split shows the game count, not "—"; thin Over% reads "3o-2u" / "3 overs, 2 unders"; fav/dog copy no longer points at a Games column that does not exist; team page tiles follow the board's rule; run/puck "What's good" leads with direction; section headings at body size; sheet title is a header. Declined: the labelled Details row's downward-only slop (the 12pt slop is audit M18, pinned by `tests/test_mobile_a11y.py`, which CI's pytest-subset failed on when it was changed; the overlap with the chips was never measured on a device); one naming scheme on the MLB/NHL chip row ("Run Line%" beside "RL% Home") — the long form on every split chip widens the scrolling row and each hint spells it out.

**Not done.** Fav/dog sample counts need new `team_stats_board` columns (a rebuild of the tuned compute function and its cache). The production effects (NBA rows, the run-line patch, the cache) land on the first 6am run after merge and were not observed.

**Session summary (2026-11-02 — NCAAB first-half and team-total purchase at three game-day times; code only):**

mike approved the spend on 2026-11-02: a test buy of about 50 games at 15:00 UTC on game day, then, if books post first-half lines by then, the full 15:00 UTC buy for 2023-24 to 2025-26 (at most 687,686 credits); 18:00 UTC and 60 minutes before the tip follow on 2026-12-01. This change is the code only. It buys nothing; the purchases run later as worker jobs.

**What changed.** `data/ingestors/ncaab_odds_history.py --derivatives` takes three new times: `15z` (15:00 UTC on the game's ESPN date, only games that tip after it), `18z` (18:00 UTC, the same rule) and `tip60` (60 minutes before the tip, down to five minutes). Each stamps its own source (`odds_api_ncaab_derivative_15z`, `_18z`, `_tip60`), resumes on its own, and is protected from the odds clean-up. Markets, endpoint, credit meter, ceiling, floor, empty-answer handling and the per-date event list are the close and open purchase's own. `--sample N` buys N games chosen by season and Eastern tip time (15 groups, each ordered by a fixed hash of the game id, taking turns; only games with a stored close), then prints, per book and market, how many of the N hold a quote at the time beside how many hold one at the close, read back from `odds`. Without `--apply` a game-day run is a read-only dry run that builds no meter. Job modes in `tracking/job_queue.py`: `deriv_15z` (cap 700,000), `deriv_18z` (650,000), `deriv_tip60` (700,000), `deriv_15z_test` (2,500, 50 games); each takes `dry_run: true` with no limits. The close, open and opener grid code paths are unchanged.

**Verified.** `tests/test_ncaab_derivative_gameday.py` (61): each time for sample games, including tips at, before and without 15:00 UTC; the source each stamps; the sampler's spread and determinism; the report's table; the jobs' command lines and refusals; and that a dry run makes no HTTP call. Watched fail: with the dry run swapped for a real buy, the four dry-run tests failed on "an HTTP call in a dry run"; with the 15:00 UTC skip removed, ten tests failed. The test buy's dry run against production, read only: 50 games on 45 dates, at most 2,045 credits, 3 or 4 games in each of the 15 groups; the stored close holds a quote for all 50.

**Calls side by side (same change, mike: "why taking so long ... I want it all done").** The opener grid and every first-half purchase send up to 6 paid calls at once (job arg `concurrency`, 1 to 10; `--concurrency`). Only the HTTP calls run in a thread pool (`PoolMeter.fetch`); writes, parsing, the keepers and the credit count stay on the main thread, and answers are handled in the order asked, so the stored rows match the one-at-a-time run (`tests/test_ncaab_paid_calls.py` compares both on a mocked feed: same rows, same order, same ledger). Before every call the meter counts each call in flight at its most (30 a snapshot, 40 a game), so calls in flight cannot cross the 300,000 floor or the job's limit; while the balance is unknown, one call at a time. A 429 pauses every call (2 s doubling to 30 s), is retried, and lowers the number in flight; the report counts them. A job whose stored args name no concurrency runs at 6, so the grid's job 936020, rerun after the redeploy, picks it up; its stored args validate. The grid's read-only dry run at 23:43Z counted 11,825 times already bought, between the ledgers' 11,821 and 11,826 rows over that read, so a rerun buys none of them again. Watched fail: with the calls in flight left out of the meter's check, four tests failed (the floor and the ceiling crossed).

**Not done.** No worker job queued, no declared job added, nothing bought. The same snapshot is paid twice where two times coincide (1,683 games at 18:00 UTC and 60 minutes before a 2pm Eastern tip; 120 at 15:00 UTC and 60 minutes before an 11am tip); left as is, for mike before 2026-12-01.

**Session summary (2026-11-02 — alt lines: pass uses the daily kill switch, kickoff holds the leg):**

Follow-up to #1089 (squash `e693060d`). CE review 5731582053, M4 and M1. Matt: at kickoff keep an alt-line betslip leg and mark it stale. No threshold, stake, or pause change. No migration.

**M4.** `buy_allowance()` in `data/ingestors/alt_game_lines.py` is the one check. `ALT_GAME_LINES_ENABLED` not `"1"` buys nothing on the daily run and on every odds pass. `ALT_GAME_LINES_MAX_GAMES` unset means no cap; a whole number caps event-odds calls for that process, sport order then game id; anything else buys nothing and fails the daily step. The pass logs that reason and still stores main lines. `FETCH_ALT_GAME_LINES=0` stays an extra gate on the pass only. Both have to allow the buy before a pass spends. `drop_started_ladders` still runs on the daily step only.

The slip keeps a leg whose only price is that ladder, struck through, under “Not in this price”, with “Pre-game price — the game has started”. The user removes it. That price is not in the parlay, the book row, or a save. A failed ladder read after kickoff holds the leg with no odds instead of deleting it. The bar stays up and says “No live price” when that is the only leg. Sheet copy says “pre-game alternate lines”, not “this morning’s”.

**Verified.** New pass tests were red (the pass still bought with the kill switch off, a bad cap, and no shared cap), then green. `tests/test_alternate_game_lines.py` and `tests/test_alt_game_lines.py`: 33 passed, 5 skipped (scratch Postgres). `verify_line_legs.ts` and `verify_betslip.ts` ALL PASS. `tsc --noEmit`: 0 diagnostics not in `tsc-baseline.txt`. One stale baseline entry on `LatestDkOddsRow` (not this change) warns and does not fail. UX review: two passes; the should-fix items were applied, including hiding FAV/DOG when there is no price. `#FFFFFFB8` on the bar’s price labels is pre-existing and was left. The extra Clear on the all-stale slip has 4pt of vertical hit slop so it meets the 44pt target; the original Clear stays on the short-button allowlist. `verify_a11y.ts` ALL PASS.

**Not done.** Mid-day ladders still sit in `alt_game_lines` until the next daily run. The app does not list them.

**Follow-up the same day (CE review of #1115).** After kickoff `buildLeg` was still copying every row at the main number into `bookPrices`, including another book's pre-game alt rung, and could credit DraftKings from a ladder row. `gameLineHold` now prices a started main-number leg from main rows only. The ladder is used only when no main row prices the leg, and then only to hold the alt. `verify_line_legs.ts`: "after kickoff a main-number leg does not pick up pre-game alt rungs" was red (FanDuel's alt link was in `bookPrices`), then green. **Session summary (2026-11-01 — app_timings CE Mediums from #1110):**

Follow-up to #1110 (merged as `6fe2db4a`, reviewed at tip `36a948fd`). Telemetry only: no model, threshold, stake, or pause change. No production write. The cap migration is hand-applied and was not run.

**M1.** `settle` in `mobile/src/lib/perfLog.ts` closed the launch window as screen `unknown` when auth/access/hydration finished and the network was quiet for 300 ms before navigation named the first screen. That launch row stopped at those early reads, and the real first screen opened a normal window. The launch window now stays open until it has a real screen name. Naming it restarts the join clock and, if the pre-nav reads have already finished, arms the quiet timer so the first screen's reads still join. `mobile/scripts/verify_perf_log.ts` covers that race. Watched failing first: the buffer held a launch row named `unknown` at 280 ms, and the later `Tabs` screen had no launch row.

**M2.** `data/app_timings_retention.py` deletes rows older than 45 days, in batches of 5,000. The daily pipeline runs it after the odds prune, and skips it on `--dry-run`. `python run_pipeline.py --step prune-app-timings` runs it alone. A missing table is a skip, not a red daily. `data/migrations/app_timings_cap_2026_11_01.sql` (not in `ACTIVE_MIGRATIONS`) adds nullable `user_id` and replaces `log_app_timings`: a signed-in caller is capped on `auth.uid()`, the count and the insert share an advisory lock, one call inserts at most the room left under 1,000, and `ms` / `status` / `request_count` / `since_launch_ms` are read as text and clamped. An anonymous caller is still capped only on the client device id. That rotation hole is unchanged; retention is what bounds the table. The 1,000 cap was not lowered, because a member who leaves the Live tab open is the population this table measures.

**Lows taken.** A batch the server rejects (HTTP 4xx or a 5-character SQLSTATE) is dropped instead of backing off forever; a network error still retries. The hourly overshoot and the uncapped integers are the cap migration above. Both hand migrations are named in `docs/app_timings.md`; neither was added to `ACTIVE_MIGRATIONS`, because the worker re-runs that list and this DDL reloads PostgREST's schema cache. `tests/test_app_timings.py` is on the pytest-subset list, which already installs Node 22, so the node checks run there. The skip for a machine without Node 22.6 stays.

**Not done.** `app_timings_cap_2026_11_01.sql` is not applied. Until it is, production still caps on device id only, and `user_id` does not exist. No App Store Connect change.
